California Real Estate Salesperson Examination: Master Practice Set (Pages 5–20)
Topics Covered: Legal Licenses, The -OR/-EE Rule, Bundle of Rights, Real vs. Personal Property, Fixtures & MARIA, Trade Fixtures, Appurtenances, Encroachments, Emblements, Water Rights, Freehold Estates, Life Estates, Leasehold Estates, Eviction Doctrines, Property Management Principles, and Commercial Lease Structures.
Question 1
A commercial developer verbally agreed to let an adjoining landholder park heavy construction machinery on a vacant corner lot for three months without requiring any rent or monetary consideration; however, six weeks later, after entering into a formal contract of sale with an institutional investor, the developer abruptly instructed the neighbor to remove all vehicles within forty-eight hours. Regarding the neighbor's legal rights and status, which of the following statements is TRUE?一家商業開發商口頭同意讓鄰地所有權人將重型工程機具停放在一處閒置的街角空地上為期三個月，且不收取任何租金或金錢對價；然而六週後，在與一家機構投資人簽訂正式的買賣契約後，該開發商突然要求鄰居在 48 小時內將所有車輛移走。關於該鄰居的法律權利與法律地位，下列哪一項敘述是正確的？ 
(A) The verbal agreement constituted an irrevocable appurtenant easement that runs with the land and burdens the developer’s grantee.
(B) The arrangement formed a personal license that created no interest in real property and remained freely revocable by the landowner at any time.
(C) The neighbor acquired an estate at will which statutorily mandates a minimum thirty-day formal written notice prior to termination.
(D) The agreement established an executed easement in gross that cannot be extinguished without compensating the neighbor for detrimental reliance.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 許可權（License）並非土地的實質所有權或權益（Not an estate or interest in land），僅屬於進入並使用他人土地的「個人特權（Personal privilege）」。許可權可由口頭合約成立、通常不具對價，且地主得隨時單方予以撤銷（Freely revocable），亦不具附隨土地移轉之效力（Does not run with the land）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 附屬地役權（Appurtenant easement）必須具備書面形式（Statute of Frauds），且屬於物權負擔，不得僅憑無償口頭約定成立。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 隨意租賃（Estate at will）屬於租賃權（Leasehold estate），其成立須有租賃合意與排他佔有權限，口頭停車特權僅屬單純許可（License）。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 個人地役權（Easement in gross）同樣屬於實質不動產權益且須為書面；無償口頭許可無法因地主隨後出售土地而自動晉升為不可撤銷之地役權。
Question 2
In standard real estate transactions involving purchase agreements, secured debt instruments, and options, the legal application of the "-OR" and "-EE" rule designates the party initiating or conveying the right versus the party receiving it. In this legal context, which of the following pairings is INCORRECT?
在涉及買賣契約、擔保債務工具以及選擇權的標準不動產交易中，「-OR」與「-EE」字尾的法律適用原則，是用來界定發起／讓與權利的一方與收受權利的一方。在這樣的法律語境下，下列哪一組配對是錯誤的？ 
(A) Trustor — The borrower pledging title to secure a promissory note.
(B) Optionor — The prospective buyer receiving the contractual right to purchase.
(C) Mortgagor — The property owner giving a security pledge to a financial lender.
(D) Grantor — The property owner executing a deed of conveyance to transfer real property.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 在選擇權契約（Option Contract）中，屋主/賣方是「給予購買權利的一方」，字尾為 -OR，稱為 Optionor（選擇權授與人）；潛在買方是「接受購買權利的一方」，字尾為 -EE，稱為 Optionee（選擇權受領人）。選項 (B) 將 Optionor 定義為買方，配對錯誤，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 信託委託人（Trustor）是借款人，將產權信託轉移給受託人（Trustee）以擔保債務。
干擾選項 (C) 正確： 抵押人（Mortgagor）是借款人，將房屋抵押權「給予/設定」給放款機構（Mortgagee）。
干擾選項 (D) 正確： 讓與人（Grantor）是簽署契據將產權移轉給受讓人（Grantee）的原屋主。
Question 3
While real property ownership is legally defined as a comprehensive "bundle of rights" encompassing possession, enjoyment, exclusion, disposition, and control, these rights are neither absolute nor permanent. With respect to legal limitations imposed on an owner's bundle of rights, all of the following statements are true, EXCEPT:
不動產所有權在法律上被定義為涵蓋佔有、收益、排除、處分與控制的完整「權利束」，但這些權利既非絕對、亦非永久。關於對所有權人權利束所施加的法律限制，下列敘述皆正確，惟除了：
(A) The recording of a mechanic's lien temporarily suspends specific disposition privileges within the bundle until the underlying debt is satisfied.
(B) The bundle of rights guarantees the owner the affirmative legal authority to refuse to exercise any individual stick within the bundle.
(C) Fee simple ownership grants absolute immunity from governmental sovereign police power and local subdivision controls.
(D) An encumbrance voluntarily executed by the property owner, such as a deed of trust, diminishes the unencumbered value of the bundle.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 產權所有人的權利束（Bundle of Rights）恆受政府四大公權力（PETE：Police Power 警察權、Eminent Domain 徵收權、Taxation 課稅權、Escheat 充公權）及私人合法物權負擔的限制，絕不存在免除政府警察權或土地規劃管制的「絕對豁免權」。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 技工留置權（Mechanic's lien）屬於法定物權負擔，在清償註銷前會限制產權處分與乾淨產權的轉讓。
干擾選項 (B) 正確： 權利束不僅包含行使各項權益（使用、出租、轉讓等）的自由，亦包含「拒絕行使（Refuse to exercise）」該等權利的自由。
干擾選項 (D) 正確： 屋主自願設定之信託契據（Deed of trust）屬於自願性物權負擔（Voluntary encumbrance），會使實質未負擔資產淨值相應減少。
Question 4
Prior to the close of escrow for a luxury hillside home, the seller entered into an unrecorded agreement to sell the mature ornamental hardwood trees situated on the estate, stipulating that the timber company could cut and remove the lumber thirty days after title conveyance. At the same time, the seller transported hundreds of loose paving stones to the backyard to construct a patio, but never laid them down. Regarding the legal classification of these items, which of the following is CORRECT?
在一棟豪華山坡住宅完成履約保證交屋前，賣方簽署了一份未經登記的協議，出售該莊園上的成熟觀賞硬木，約定木材公司可在產權移轉後三十日內砍伐並運走木材。同時，賣方將數百塊鬆散鋪石運至後院準備建造露臺，但從未鋪設。關於這些物品的法律定性，下列何者正確？
(A) The standing trees converted immediately to personalty via annexation upon executing the lumber contract.
(B) The loose paving stones became fixtures automatically under the doctrine of constructive annexation.
(C) The trees remain real property until physically severed from the land, while the unlaid paving stones remain personal property.
(D) Both the standing timber and the unlaid paving stones are legally classified as fructus industriales.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 未經砍伐之天然樹木為土地附著物，在實體砍伐分離（Severance）前仍屬不動產（Real Property）；散落於後院、尚未鋪設水泥固定的石磚，因欠缺實質附著（Annexation）與整合，仍屬可移動之動產（Personal Property / Chattels）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 砍樹使不動產轉化為動產的過程稱為「分離（Severance）」，而非「附著（Annexation）」，且在未砍伐前物權本質仍為不動產。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 散落建材未經實體安裝施工，不符合定著物判定標準（MARIA）。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 天然樹木屬於天然收益（Fructus naturales），人工定期耕作之農作物才屬於勞動收益（Fructus industriales / Emblements）。
Question 5
A dispute arose between the purchaser of a historic residential dwelling and the seller regarding a custom-built, antique stained-glass dining room chandelier. The purchase contract remained completely silent regarding the light fixture; however, the fixture had been hardwired directly into the electrical circuitry, custom-adapted to the dining ceiling molding, and hung by heavy metal chains screwed into the main ceiling joists. If the matter is litigated under the MARIA test, which factor will the court view as the ULTIMATE deciding element in the absence of an express contract clause?
歷史住宅的買方與賣方就一盞訂製古董彩繪玻璃餐廳吊燈發生爭議。買賣契約對該燈具完全未作約定；但該燈具已直接硬接電路、依餐廳天花板線板客製安裝，並以重金屬鏈鎖入主樑。若依 MARIA 測試進行訴訟，在欠缺明示契約條款時，法院將以哪一項作為最終決定因素？
(A) The adaptability of the chandelier to the specific architectural design of the room.
(B) The objective intention of the annexor at the time the fixture was permanently installed.
(C) The high historical monetary cost and sentimental appraisal value of the antique fixture.
(D) The specific relationship of the parties favoring the seller over the buyer.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 判定定著物的 MARIA 測試法中，當書面買賣契約（Agreement）未作明文約定時，法院最核心且具決定性的法律標準為「安裝附著時的客觀意圖（Intention of the annexor）」，並輔以附著方式（Method）與專用適應性（Adaptability）來佐證該意圖。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 適應性（Adaptability）雖為 MARIA 測試要素之一，但主要用於佐證當初安裝者的客觀永久留置意圖。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 物品的「原始購置成本」或「主觀情感價值」完全不是 MARIA 測試法的法定判斷標準。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 在當事人關係（Relationship of the parties）的法律推定中，法院在買賣爭議中通常「偏向保護買方（Favor buyer over seller）」，而非偏向賣方。
Question 6
A high-end retail apparel tenant leased space in a major regional shopping center under a five-year commercial lease. During tenant improvements, the retailer bolted bespoke clothing display racks to the hardwood flooring and recessed specialized high-intensity halogen spotlights into the acoustic ceiling. Two weeks prior to lease expiration, the tenant began dismantling these items. The commercial landlord filed for an injunction, asserting the items converted to real property through permanent physical annexation. Which of the following legal determinations is CORRECT?
一家高檔服飾零售租戶依五年商業租約承租大型區域購物中心空間。裝潢時，零售商將訂製展示架鎖入硬木地板，並將高強度鹵素聚光燈嵌進吸音天花板。租期屆滿前兩週，租戶開始拆除這些物品。商業房東聲請禁制令，主張該等物品已因永久實體附著轉為不動產。下列法律判斷何者正確？
(A) The spotlights and display cases became permanent fixtures that belong exclusively to the landlord without compensation.
(B) The items are trade fixtures classified as personal property, which the tenant may remove prior to lease termination provided any damage is restored.
(C) The fixtures converted to appurtenances running with the underlying commercial real estate under the doctrine of accession.
(D) The tenant may remove the display racks only if the landlord provides prior written authorization and pays fair salvage value.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 商業租戶為經營業務或貿易所需而安裝的設備屬於「營業用定著物（Trade Fixtures）」。在法律上，營業定著物仍保留為承租人的「動產（Personal property）」，承租人有權在租期屆滿前將其拆除移走，但負有修復因拆除造成之實體損害（Restore damages）的法定義務。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 營業用設備不因鎖入地板而喪失動產屬性，並未自動撥歸房東所有。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 從屬物（Appurtenance）是隨土地移轉之附屬權益或建物，並非商業租客之營業設備。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 租客拆除自身合法動產設備為法定權利，無須取得房東同意，房東亦無義務支付殘值。
Question 7
A rural property owner transferred fee title of a twenty-acre agricultural parcel to a buyer via a standard grant deed. Situated on the property was an old detached wooden granary, a recorded access road easement across the adjacent westerly tract, and subterranean mineral rights. The deed contained no explicit mention of the granary, the westerly access easement, or the subsurface mineral rights. Following the closing of escrow, which of the following statements accurately reflects the legal status of these items?
鄉村地主以標準授與契據將二十英畝農地的完全產權移轉給買方。該地有一棟獨立舊木穀倉、跨越西側鄰地已登記之通行地役權，以及地下礦物權。契據未明示提及穀倉、西側通行地役權或地下礦物權。履約保證完成後，下列何者正確反映這些項目的法律地位？
(A) The easement and granary automatically conveyed to the buyer as appurtenances running with the land, despite being omitted from the deed text.
(B) The grantor retained exclusive subsurface mineral rights under the legal presumption that all subterranean rights require express deed conveyance.
(C) The access road easement was extinguished by merger because it was not specifically re-granted within the four corners of the instrument.
(D) The granary remained the personal property of the grantor because unattached outbuildings are treated legally as personalty.
【正確答案】 (A)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (A) 解析： 從屬物與從屬權利（Appurtenance）包含附著於土地之建築物（如穀倉）、附屬地役權（Appurtenant easements）及地下礦物權利。從屬物具備「附隨土地移轉（Runs with the land）」之法律效力，即使契據（Deed）內文未逐一明載，亦隨主體土地所有權之移轉自動移轉給受讓人（Grantee）。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 除非讓與人在契據中明確設定「保留條款（Reservation）」，否則所有地下權利隨產權一併移轉。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 附屬地役權不會因契據未提及而消滅，混同（Merger）僅發生於需役地與供役地所有權歸同一人所有時。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 固定座落於土地之穀倉屬於不動產定著物/建築物，並非動產。
Question 8
A professional land survey performed during a pending residential escrow revealed that the neighboring owner had erected an ornamental stone retaining wall and concrete driveway extension that physically intruded three feet across the boundary line onto the seller’s parcel. Under California real property law, how is this physical intrusion classified, and what is its legal nature?
在進行中的住宅履約保證期間，專業土地測量顯示鄰地所有權人興建的觀賞石擋土牆與混凝土車道延伸，實體侵入賣方地界三英尺。依加州不動產法，此種實體侵入如何定性，其法律性質為何？
(A) An appurtenant easement that automatically converted into an incorporeal hereditament upon discovery.
(B) An encroachment, which constitutes a physical intrusion upon the land and represents a form of continuous actionable trespass.
(C) An implied license that grants the neighbor permanent equitable servitude over the affected boundary strip.
(D) An emblement that entitles the encroaching neighbor to ongoing seasonal exploitation under the doctrine of estvers.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 建築物、改良物或圍牆車道未經授權跨越相鄰地界線之實體侵入，稱為「越界侵占（Encroachment）」。在法律性質上，越界侵占構成持續性的「非法侵入（Trespass）」，通常經由專業土地測量（Survey）發現，受害地主得請求法院核發禁制令排除或請求損害賠償。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 未經授權之實體越界並非合法創設的地役權（Easement）。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 越界侵占並非許可（License），亦非衡平地役負擔（Equitable servitude）。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 收益動產（Emblements）專指年度人工耕作農作物，與建築構造物越界完全無關。
Question 9
A tenant farmer entered into a three-year agricultural lease to plant and harvest hybrid corn. In the second year of the term, the life tenant who leased the acreage suddenly passed away, instantly extinguishing the underlying leasehold estate two months prior to harvest. The remainderman took immediate possession and barred the farmer from entering the fields, claiming full ownership of the standing unharvested corn crops. Under the common law doctrine of emblements, which of the following statements is CORRECT?
一位佃農簽訂三年農業租約種植並收穫雜交玉米。租期第二年，出租該農地的終身產權人突然過世，使租賃權立刻消滅，且距收成尚有兩個月。剩餘權人立即佔有並禁止農夫進入田地，主張未收割的玉米全歸其所有。依普通法農作物孳息原則（emblements），下列敘述何者正確？
(A) The crops became real property adhering to the remainderman’s estate upon the life tenant’s unexpected demise.
(B) The tenant farmer maintains the legal right to enter the parcel to cultivate and harvest that season’s annual crops as personal property.
(C) The farmer forfeited all rights to the corn because an agricultural lease automatically creates an estate at sufferance upon lessor death.
(D) The remainderman may confiscate the crops provided the estate reimburses the farmer for baseline seed costs.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 依據「農作物收益權法則（Doctrine of Emblements）」，由承租農民經由勞動人工耕種之年度農作物（Fructus Industriales）在法律上被視為農民的「動產（Personal property）」。若租約因不可預見之情事（如終身權人非因農民過錯而身故）提前終止，承租農民依法享有在該生長季結束前，重新進入土地照料並採收農作物的默示法定權利。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 年度人工農作物不因土地所有權回歸而轉化為地主的不動產。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 農民並未構成容忍租賃（Estate at sufferance），且 Emblements 法則正是為保障農民勞動成果而設。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 剩餘權人（Remainderman）無權沒收農作物，農民享有完整採收處分權。
Question 10
Regarding the legal doctrines governing water rights and the physical alterations caused by water movement across real property, which of the following statements is LEAST likely correct?
關於規範水權以及水流穿越不動產所造成實體變更的法律原則，下列哪一項最不可能正確？
(A) Riparian rights govern water usage for property owners whose land borders flowing watercourses such as rivers and streams.
(B) Littoral rights govern water usage for landowners whose properties border non-flowing, navigable bodies of water such as lakes and oceans.
(C) Avulsion refers to the slow, imperceptible accumulation of soil upon a shoreline resulting in the gradual enlargement of the riparian parcel.
(D) Under the doctrine of correlative use, a landowner holds the reasonable right to extract subterranean percolating water for agricultural irrigation.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 水流緩慢、漸進沉積土壤使土地面積增加之現象稱為 沖積（Accretion）（沉積物稱為 Alluvium）；崩蝕（Avulsion） 則是指因暴洪、水壩決堤或地震等突發且劇烈的自然外力造成土地瞬間被撕裂流失之現象。選項 (C) 定義錯誤，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 河流/溪流等流動水體之水權稱為河流沿岸水權（Riparian Rights，記憶點：Riparian = River）。
干擾選項 (B) 正確： 湖泊/海洋等非流動水體之水權稱為湖泊海洋水權（Littoral Rights，記憶點：Littoral = Lake）。
干擾選項 (D) 正確： 關聯使用權（Correlative Use）允許地主在合理範圍內抽取地下水或河水進行灌溉。
Question 11
In an arid Western jurisdiction governed by statutory water allocation rather than traditional common law riparian riparianism, a commercial rancher purchased a parcel bordering a natural stream and immediately diverted seventy percent of the river’s volume for private mining operations, entirely depriving downstream agricultural farms established twenty years earlier. The downstream farmers filed an injunction citing the Doctrine of Prior Appropriation. Under this doctrine, how will the dispute be adjudicated?
在一處受法定水資源分配（而非傳統普通法河岸權）規範的乾旱西部司法管轄區，商業牧場主購入臨天然溪流的地塊，立刻截取河川流量七成供私人採礦，使二十年前即已設立的下游農場合然無水。下游農夫依「先佔用水原則」（Doctrine of Prior Appropriation）聲請禁制令。依該原則，爭議將如何裁決？
(A) The commercial rancher prevails because physical adjacency to flowing water grants paramount riparian entitlements over non-adjacent properties.
(B) The downstream farmers prevail because water rights are allocated chronologically based on the historical date of beneficial use established by state permit.
(C) The dispute is settled by dividing the water volume equally between all adjoining landowners under correlative apportionment.
(D) The rancher prevails provided the water diversion does not reduce the navigable depth required for commercial maritime transit.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 在乾旱缺水地區適用的「先佔先得水權原則（Doctrine of Prior Appropriation）」，水權之分配並非取決於土地是否與水體相鄰（Geographic adjacency），而是由州政府依據申請取得許可並投入「有益用途（Beneficial use）」的「時間先後順序（Chronological priority）」來決定（即 First in time, first in right）。下游農民建立有益使用在先，享有優先上位水權。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 在 Prior Appropriation 管轄下，傳統河流沿岸相鄰權（Riparian rights）已被法規優先原則取代。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 關聯均分原則（Correlative use）適用於地下水等共同合理使用，非 Prior Appropriation 爭端裁決方式。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 航行權非先佔水權之核心判定基準，上游無權剝奪既有核准之有益用水配額。
Question 12
A grantor executed a fee simple deed conveying a historic parcel to a municipal school district stating: "To the City School District so long as the property is used exclusively for educational public instruction; but if ever used for commercial warehousing, the title shall instantly revert to the grantor, his heirs, or assigns." Five years later, the school board leased the property to a private logistics firm for freight staging. What specific estate was conveyed, and what is the current legal status of the title?
讓與人以完全產權契據將一歷史地塊讓與市立學區，載明：「予市立學區，惟該不動產須專供公共教育教學使用；若改作商業倉儲，產權即刻回歸讓與人、其繼承人或不特定受讓人。」五年後，教育委員會將該地出租予私人物流公司作為貨運調度場。當初讓與的是何種產權，目前產權法律狀態為何？
(A) Fee simple absolute; the district retains unrestricted fee title because deed restraints against commercial leases are void.
(B) Fee simple subject to a condition subsequent; the grantor must affirmatively file a quiet title lawsuit to exercise the right of entry.
(C) Fee simple determinable; title automatically reverted to the original grantor immediately upon the breach of the limitation.
(D) Life estate pur autre vie; title vested in the municipal board for the measuring life of the city mayor.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 契據中使用「只要...（So long as / While / During）」等限定詞，且明定違反時產權「立即自動回歸（Automatically revert）」者，屬於 限定決定可廢止產權（Fee Simple Determinable）。一旦指定事件或限制條件遭到違反，所有權無須經由司法訴訟或讓與人主張進入，即在法律上「自動終止並回歸」原讓與人或其繼承人。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 該契據附帶明確限制條件，非完全無條件所有權（Fee simple absolute）。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 解除條件產權（Fee simple subject to a condition subsequent）使用 "provided that / on condition that"，且違反應由讓與人主動採取行動行使重新進入權（Right of re-entry），不會自動復權。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 題幹並非以某人之生命存續期為限，非終身產權（Life estate）。
Question 13
An elderly homeowner executed a valid grant deed conveying a residential property to his adult son, "reserving unto the father a Life Estate for the duration of the father's natural life, with the remainder vesting in fee simple in the grantor’s daughter upon the father's death." During his occupancy, the father mortgaged the property with a private lender and entered into a written four-year lease with a residential tenant. Which of the following legal outcomes is CORRECT regarding these encumbrances?
年長屋主以有效授與契據將住宅產權讓與成年兒子，「為父親保留其有生之年的終身產權，父親過世後剩餘權以完全產權歸屬於讓與人之女兒。」佔有期間，父親以該屋向私人放貸人設定抵押，並與住宅租戶簽訂四年書面租約。關於這些負擔，下列法律結果何者正確？
(A) The private mortgage lien and the residential lease automatically terminate the moment the father dies.
(B) The daughter takes fee title upon the father's death subject to the mandatory fulfillment of the remaining lease term.
(C) The daughter becomes personally liable for the outstanding mortgage balance executed by her late father under the doctrine of remainder accession.
(D) The conveyance of the life estate is void ab initio because life tenants lack the legal capacity to execute leases or mortgages.
【正確答案】 (A)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (A) 解析： 終身權人（Life Tenant）在自身生命存續期間（Measuring life）享有佔有、出租或抵押物業之權限；然而，其所能處分之權益上限不得超越其自身終身權限。因此，終身權人簽訂之任何租約或抵押設定，於其「身故之瞬間自動隨之消滅（Automatically terminate upon death）」，不得拘束後續取得完全產權之剩餘權人（Remainderman）。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 剩餘權人（女兒）取得完全產權時不受原租約拘束，租約已隨生命計量人之身故終止。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 女兒未簽署借據，亦非抵押主體，不承擔任何個人清償責任，抵押權隨終身產權一同消滅。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 終身權人具備完全行為能力得在其存續期內簽署租賃與抵押合約。
Question 14
A life tenant holding a residential life estate pur autre vie measured by the life of a third-party invalid cousin deliberately neglected property maintenance, failed to pay municipal property taxes for three consecutive years, and began felling a protected grove of heritage oak trees to sell as commercial firewood. Which legal remedy is the remainderman entitled to pursue against the life tenant?
持有以第三方病弱表親之生命為衡量的終身產權（pur autre vie）之人，故意疏於維護、連續三年未繳市政地產稅，並砍伐受保護的古櫟樹林作為商業柴薪出售。剩餘權人得對終身產權人主張何種法律救濟？
(A) File an action for constructive eviction to declare the life estate converted into a periodic tenancy.
(B) File a legal action for waste, seeking monetary damages and an injunction to halt the physical and economic destruction of the reversionary interest.
(C) Demand immediate acceleration of the underlying fee simple promissory note under the alienation clause.
(D) Petition the local zoning board to execute an inverse condemnation against the life tenant.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 終身權人對物業負有繳納日常稅捐、維持屋況之法定義務，且「嚴禁造成損耗/毀損（Prohibited from committing waste）」。砍伐保護林木、蓄意荒廢物業及欠稅損及物業價值與未來剩餘權人之權益，構成法律上的 毀損物業（Waste），剩餘權人有權向法院起訴請求損害賠償並聲請禁制令（Injunctive relief）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 推定驅逐（Constructive eviction）是租賃法中租客對抗房東違法不作為之救濟手段。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 讓與條款（Alienation clause）為借款融資合約條款，與終身權人的毀損責任無關。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 反向徵收（Inverse condemnation）是私有地主向政府索賠之訴訟，非私人侵權救濟。
Question 15
Regarding the fundamental legal distinctions between Freehold Estates and Less Than Freehold (Leasehold) Estates, all of the following comparisons are accurate, EXCEPT:
關於自由保有產權（Freehold Estates）與低於自由保有之租賃產權（Leasehold Estates）的根本法律區別，下列比較皆正確，惟除了：
(A) Freehold estates are characterized by an ownership interest of indefinite or unpredictable duration.
(B) Less than freehold estates represent possessory interests held for a definite, fixed, or determinable period of time.
(C) Both freehold estates and leasehold estates are legally classified under California civil law as real property chattels.
(D) The holder of a fee simple estate possesses the underlying title, whereas a leasehold tenant holds personal property rights termed chattels real.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 自由繼承產權（Freehold estates）在法律上屬於實質的「不動產所有權（Real Property）」；而租賃權（Leasehold / Less than freehold estates）本質上屬於動產性質的「動產不動產化權益 / 不動產動產（Chattels real / Personal property）」。將兩者皆歸類為動產是完全錯誤的，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 自由產權（如 Fee simple 或 Life estate）之存續期間均為不確定或不可預期之期間（Indefinite duration）。
干擾選項 (B) 正確： 非自由產權（租賃權）之佔有使用具有確定或可判定之期限。
干擾選項 (D) 正確： 完全產權人持有 Real Property 產權，而租客持有的是租賃動產權益（Chattels real）。
Question 16
A tenant entered into a written lease for a commercial warehouse beginning on June 1, 2026, and explicitly terminating at midnight on November 30, 2026. On November 15, 2026, the landlord demanded that the tenant vacate immediately, asserting that because the landlord had not served a thirty-day notice to quit, the lease converted into a month-to-month tenancy. The tenant refused to vacate prior to November 30. Under landlord-tenant law, how is this leasehold classified, and what notice was legally required to end it on November 30?
租戶簽訂商業倉庫書面租約，自 2026 年 6 月 1 日起至 2026 年 11 月 30 日午夜明示終止。2026 年 11 月 15 日，房東要求租戶立刻遷出，主張因未送達三十日終止通知，租約已轉為按月租賃。租戶拒絕於 11 月 30 日前遷出。依房東租戶法，此租賃權如何定性，於 11 月 30 日終止依法須為何種通知？
(A) Periodic tenancy; requiring forty-five days advance written statutory notice from either party.
(B) Tenancy at sufferance; requiring judicial process and a three-day notice to pay or quit.
(C) Estate for years; terminating automatically upon the arrival of the agreed expiration date without any advance notice.
(D) Estate at will; requiring reasonable verbal notice given the commercial character of the premises.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 凡具有「確定開始與結束日期（Definite start and end date）」的租賃合約，不論期間長短（數月或數年），皆屬於 定期租賃（Estate for Years / Tenancy for Years）。定期租賃於約定期滿日「自動終止（Self-terminates）」，無須任何一方發出提前解約通知（No notice required）。租客在 11 月 30 日前享有合法佔有權。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 週期性租賃（Periodic tenancy）無固定結束日，按期自動滾動，才需要法定提前通知。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 容忍租賃（Tenancy at sufferance）發生在租約期滿後租客賴著不走之狀態，租期內不構成。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 隨意租賃（Estate at will）存續期不固定，本案有明確約定起訖日。
Question 17
A residential tenant leased an apartment under a written month-to-month rental agreement. After continuously occupying the premises and paying rent on the first of every month for two years, the tenant decided to relocate to another city. Regarding the legal requirements to effectively terminate this periodic tenancy, which of the following is CORRECT?
住宅租戶依書面按月租約承租公寓。連續佔有並於每月一日繳租兩年後，租戶決定遷往他市。關於有效終止此定期租賃的法律要件，下列何者正確？
(A) The lease terminates automatically at the conclusion of the calendar month without any notice requirements.
(B) The tenant must provide proper statutory advance written notice to the landlord prior to terminating the periodic tenancy.
(C) The tenant may terminate immediately without notice provided the security deposit is surrendered as liquidated damages.
(D) The death of either the landlord or the tenant immediately and automatically extinguishes the periodic tenancy by operation of law.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 按月續約之 週期性租賃（Periodic Tenancy） 在法律上會自動連續遞延進入下一個週期，若任一方欲終止租約，必須依法提前給予書面解約通知（Statutory advance written notice）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 期滿自動終止且不需通知的是定期租賃（Estate for years），而非週期性租賃。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 押金（Security deposit）依法不可作為任意免除法定解約通知義務的違約金。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 週期性租賃不因房東或房客之死亡而自動終止，其租賃權益與義務轉由遺產繼承人/遺產管理人承接。
Question 18
A tenant whose one-year written residential lease expired on December 31 continued to occupy the premises throughout January without the landlord's consent and without remitting any rental payments. The landlord refused to accept further funds and initiated legal proceedings. During the period of unauthorized holdover occupancy prior to court eviction, what legal estate did the tenant hold?
租戶一年期書面住宅租約於 12 月 31 日屆滿後，未經房東同意且未繳任何租金，於整個一月繼續佔用。房東拒絕接受後續款項並提起法律程序。在法院驅逐前的無權滯留期間，租戶持有何種法律產權？
(A) Estate at will, entitling the occupant to undisturbed possession until an equitable accounting is rendered.
(B) Estate at sufferance, resulting from the tenant wrongfully retaining possession after the expiration of lawful tenancy.
(C) Unlawful trespass ab initio, subjecting the occupant to immediate summary criminal arrest without civil process.
(D) Executory life tenancy, permitting the tenant to remain until the landlord tenders formal compensation.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 當承租人於合法租約期滿後，未獲房東同意且未繳租金仍繼續非法滯留佔有物業時，該滯留租客（Holdover tenant）所持有之法律地位稱為 容忍租賃 / 轉非法人住租賃（Estate at Sufferance / Tenancy at Sufferance）。因其最初進駐時具合法權源，故非自始擅闖（Not trespass ab initio），但房東必須循民事非法居留訴訟程序（Unlawful detainer）進行驅逐，不得採取自力救濟。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 隨意租賃（Estate at will）必須建立在房東允許或合意佔有的基礎上。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 由於最初為合法進駐，民法上不構成自始刑事擅闖，警察不得在無法院驅逐令下直接刑事逮捕。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 超期滯留完全不產生終身產權或補償請求權。
Question 19
During a severe winter cold snap, the centralized heating boiler in a residential multi-family building malfunctioned. Despite four successive written notices served by the tenant over a twenty-five-day period, the landlord deliberately refused to hire a licensed technician, leaving the unit uninhabitable with indoor temperatures plunging below freezing. Unable to endure the conditions, the tenant vacated the premises entirely and surrendered the keys. Under California property jurisprudence, which legal doctrine did the tenant execute?
嚴冬寒流期間，多戶住宅大樓中央暖氣鍋爐故障。租戶於二十五日內連續送達四次書面通知，房東仍故意拒絕聘請合格技師，使室內氣溫降至冰點以下而不適居住。租戶無法忍受，完全遷出並交還鑰匙。依加州財產法理，租戶行使的是何種法律原則？
(A) Actual physical ejectment under the doctrine of eminent forfeiture.
(B) Constructive eviction, discharging the tenant from any ongoing or future rent payment liabilities.
(C) Tenancy at sufferance with an offset claim for retaliatory waste.
(D) Implied quiet title action against the landlord's reversionary estate.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 當房東嚴重違反維持物業適合人居之法定義務（如寒冬拒不修繕暖氣），導致房屋實質上無法居住，迫使租客不得不搬離並交還管轄權時，法律上構成 推定驅逐 / 實質驅逐（Constructive Eviction）。租客完成實質搬遷（Vacate）後，依法徹底免除後續所有租金支付義務，並得向房東求償。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 實體驅逐（Actual eviction）是指房東透過執法機關實體驅離租客。
干擾哪項 (C) 錯誤： 租客主動撤出係行使合法解約抗辯，並非容忍租賃。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 租客並非主張物業所有權，與確認產權之訴（Quiet title）無關。
Question 20
A commercial retail tenant holding a five-year lease negotiated a transaction whereby a third party assumed the entire unexpired four-year balance of the lease term, taking full possession of the retail unit and paying rent directly to the property owner. The landlord did not execute a novation or release. Under contract and real property law, what transaction occurred, and what is the original tenant's liability?
持有五年租約的商業零售租戶洽談一筆交易，由第三人承擔剩餘四年租期、完全佔有該店面並直接向業主繳租。房東未簽署契約更新（novation）或免除。依契約與不動產法，發生了何種交易，原租戶的責任為何？
(A) A sublease occurred; the original tenant remains primarily and solely liable to the landlord for all rent obligations.
(B) An assignment occurred; the assignee assumes primary liability, but the original tenant (assignor) remains secondarily liable.
(C) A novation occurred by implication; completely discharging the original tenant from all contractual obligations.
(D) An accord and satisfaction occurred; converting the leasehold into a unilateral license.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 將租賃合約中「所有剩餘期限與空間」完整移轉給第三人之法律行為稱為 合約轉讓（Assignment）。在轉讓後，受讓人（Assignee）對房東負主要履行責任（基於物權關係 Privity of estate），但原承租人（Assignor）基於原契約關係（Privity of contract）仍負有次要連帶保證責任（Secondarily liable），除非房東簽署新約取代（Novation）明確免除其責任。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 分租/次租（Sublease）僅移轉部分空間或部分租期，原租客對房東維持第一線主要責任。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 契約更新（Novation）必須有三方明確合意簽署，不得僅憑默示推定免責。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 和解與清償（Accord and satisfaction）為債務爭議之解決方式，與租約轉讓無關。
Question 21
Regarding the implied or express covenant of quiet enjoyment embedded in California residential and commercial leases, which of the following statements is TRUE?
關於嵌入加州住宅與商業租約中之默示或明示安靜享用契約（covenant of quiet enjoyment），下列敘述何者正確？
(A) The covenant guarantees that neighboring property owners will never create exterior noise exceeding municipal decibel thresholds.
(B) The covenant protects the tenant against wrongful physical interference or disturbance of possession by the landlord or anyone holding superior title.
(C) The covenant allows the landlord the unrestricted legal right to enter the leased premises at any hour without notice to conduct random inspections.
(D) The covenant guarantees that the tenant's commercial business operations will generate profitable financial returns during the lease term.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 安寧享有權條款（Covenant of Quiet Enjoyment） 是租賃契約的核心法定保證，確保承租人在租賃期間享有和平佔有與使用物業之權益，免受來自房東本人、其代理人或任何持有上位合法產權者（Superior title）之非法實體干擾、侵入或妨礙。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 安寧享有權並非保證社區外部完全無噪音，而是針對產權干擾與房東行為之法律保障。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 房東任意無故侵入租客居所恰恰構成對安寧享有權的實質侵犯（Breach of quiet enjoyment）。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 法律不對租客之商業獲利提供任何擔保。
Question 22
A licensed real estate broker entered into a formal written property management agreement with an absentee owner to manage a 40-unit residential apartment complex. In this capacity, which of the following correctly classifies the broker’s agency status and primary legal objective?
持照不動產經紀人與不在場業主簽訂正式書面物業管理契約，管理一棟四十戶住宅公寓。在此身分下，下列何者正確界定經紀人之代理地位與主要法律目標？
(A) Special agent; whose sole legal objective is ensuring zero residential tenant vacancies at below-market rates.
(B) Universal agent; whose objective is liquidating real property assets to achieve immediate capital appreciation.
(C) General agent; whose primary objective is maximizing net operating income while preserving and maintaining property asset value.
(D) Dual agent; whose primary fiduciary duty is representing both the property owner and the individual tenants equally.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 物業經理人（Property Manager）獲授權持續處理特定物業的日常營運與管理事務，在代理法上屬於 一般代理人（General Agent）。物業經理對屋主負有完整的受託人信託義務（Fiduciary duties），其核心營運目標為：在維持並提升物業資產價值（Preserving property value）的同時，創造最高之物業淨營運收入（Maximizing net operating income）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 房屋買賣委託經紀人為特別代理人（Special agent）；低價換取零空置率違反最大化收益目標。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 全權代理人（Universal agent）具備無限處分權（如全權委託書 POA），非一般物管合約之定位。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 物業經理係受僱於屋主，信託忠誠責任專屬於屋主，非雙重代理。
Question 23
A comprehensive risk management audit performed by a professional property manager for a multi-tenant commercial office building resulted in several operational directives. When matching these operational decisions with categorized risk management principles, which of the following pairings is INCORRECT?
專業物業管理人對多租戶商業辦公大樓進行全面風險管理稽核後，作成若干營運指示。將這些營運決定與風險管理原則配對時，下列哪一組配對是錯誤的？
(A) Installing commercial fire sprinkler systems and electronic smoke detection alarms $\rightarrow$ Risk Control.
(B) Removing a deteriorating wooden pedestrian footbridge and closing off an unsafe rooftop deck $\rightarrow$ Risk Avoidance.
(C) Purchasing an all-risk commercial hazard insurance policy carrying a substantial $25,000 deductible $\rightarrow$ Risk Retention (Dealing with risk).
(D) Purchasing a comprehensive third-party liability insurance policy with zero deductible $\rightarrow$ Risk Severance.
【正確答案】 (D)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (D) 解析： 風險管理四大策略包含：規避（Avoidance）、自留（Retention/Dealing）、控制（Control）與轉嫁（Transfer）。購買全面保險將潛在賠償責任全數移轉給保險公司承擔，屬於 風險轉嫁（Risk Transfer）；不動產法中並無所謂「風險分離（Risk Severance）」之術語（Severance 係指將不動產分離為動產之物權行為）。配對錯誤，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 安裝滅火灑水與警報器以降低災害損害程度，屬於 風險控制（Risk Control）。
干擾選項 (B) 正確： 拆除危險設施以徹底消滅危險源，屬於 風險規避（Risk Avoidance）。
干擾選項 (C) 正確： 選擇高自負額保單自行吸收小額損失，屬於 風險自留/承擔（Risk Retention / Dealing with risk）。
Question 24
Under a standard residential lease agreement where the written contract remains completely silent regarding the specific calendar due date for rental payments, when is the rent legally due and payable by the tenant under California law?
在標準住宅租約中，書面契約對租金具體到期日完全未作約定時，依加州法律，租金依法於何時到期應付？
(A) On the first business day of the designated leasing period in advance.
(B) On the fifteenth day of each calendar month under the doctrine of substantial performance.
(C) On the final calendar day of the designated leasing period.
(D) Whenever the landlord tenders a formal written demand for accounting.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 若租賃契約中未明確約定租金給付期日（Contract is silent on due date），依據普通法與加州法定租賃推定原則，租金並非預先在月初支付，而是在 該租賃週期的最後一天（Last day of the leasing period） 到期給付（因為租客享有先使用後結算收益之法理推定）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 雖實務常約定月初預付，但若合約未明載，法律不推定為期初預繳。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 月中支付無任何法定依據。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 租金給付義務依租賃週期屆滿產生，非取決於房東隨機之書面催告。
Question 25
An investor owns several commercial and residential rental assets. One property is leased under a Gross Lease, another under a Percentage Lease, and a third under a Triple Net (NNN) Lease. Regarding the operational expense allocations inherent in these lease structures, which of the following is NOT true?
投資人持有若干商業與住宅出租資產。一筆依總額租約（Gross Lease）、一筆依抽成租約（Percentage Lease）、一筆依三淨租約（NNN）。關於這些租約結構內含的營運費用分攤，下列何者不正確？
(A) Under a Gross Lease, the tenant pays a fixed flat rental sum, and the landlord remains obligated to pay all property taxes, hazard insurance, and structural repairs.
(B) Under a Triple Net (NNN) Lease, the tenant pays a base rent plus real estate taxes, property insurance, and ongoing common area maintenance costs.
(C) Under a Percentage Lease commonly used in retail centers, rent is calculated partly or entirely as a percentage of the gross sales receipts generated on the premises.
(D) Under a Triple Net (NNN) Lease, the commercial landlord retains the sole legal and financial responsibility to pay all building insurance and municipal property taxes without tenant reimbursement.
【正確答案】 (D)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (D) 解析： 在 三淨租約（Triple Net / NNN Lease） 架構下，承租人除支付固定基本租金外，契約明確約定由「承租人（Tenant）」直接負擔或向房東全額補貼所有的物業稅（Net Taxes）、建築保險費（Net Insurance）與維護營運費（Net Maintenance）。選項 (D) 聲稱房東承擔且不得要求租客補償是完全錯誤的，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 毛租約/總額租約（Gross Lease）由租客付固定租金，房東全額吸收營運維護與稅費。
干擾選項 (B) 正確： 三淨租約中租客承擔三大淨額開支（Taxes, Insurance, Maintenance）。
干擾選項 (C) 正確： 百分比/抽成租約（Percentage Lease）按租客在該商場的營業總額（Gross Sales）抽成計租。
Question 26
A tenant leased a single-family home under a written "Lease Option" agreement for a two-year term, paying a monthly rent of $3,500 along with an upfront, non-refundable cash option fee of $15,000 to the landlord. The agreement granted the tenant the exclusive right to purchase the property for $650,000 at any time prior to lease expiration. Regarding the legal nature of this agreement, which of the following is CORRECT?
租戶依書面「租賃附購買選擇權」（Lease Option）承租獨棟住宅兩年，每月租金 3,500 美元，並向房東預付不可退還的選擇權金 15,000 美元。協議賦予租戶在租期屆滿前隨時以 650,000 美元購買該屋的專屬權利。關於此協議的法律性質，下列何者正確？
(A) The contract is a bilateral agreement from inception, legally compelling the tenant to purchase the property at $650,000.
(B) The landlord is the Optionee and the tenant is the Optionor bound by a personal service employment contract.
(C) The contract begins as a unilateral contract binding only the optionor, and converts into an enforceable bilateral sales contract once the tenant exercises the option.
(D) The agreement is voidable at the option of the landlord because the $15,000 fee constitutes illegal unearned consideration under California law.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 附買回選擇權租約（Lease Option） 在簽約時為 單務契約（Unilateral Contract），房東（Optionor 授與人）受定金對價約束，承諾在期限內只要租客決定購買就必須出售；而租客（Optionee 受領人）保有買或不買的自由選擇權。一旦租客正式行使選擇權（Exercise the option），該合約立即轉變為具雙向拘束力的「雙務買賣契約（Bilateral Sales Contract）」。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 簽約初期對租客而言無強制購買義務，非雙務合約。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 房東為 Optionor（授與人），租客為 Optionee（受領人）。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 選擇權必須有實質對價（Valuable consideration）支持，非退還之 Option fee 完全合法有效。
Question 27
An owner of an unencumbered parcel of land granted an electric utility corporation the recorded right to erect high-voltage transmission towers and string power cables across the northern boundary of the property. Years later, the owner subdivided and conveyed the parcel to various residential buyers. With respect to the utility company's legal interest, which of the following statements is CORRECT?
無負擔土地所有權人授與電力公用事業已登記之權利，得於該地北界架設高壓電塔並拉設電纜。數年後，所有權人將該地分割讓與多名住宅買方。關於電力公司的法律權益，下列敘述何者正確？
(A) The utility company holds an appurtenant dominant tenement that automatically merged upon residential subdivision.
(B) The utility company holds an easement in gross, which represents a personal or commercial interest burdening the land without a benefited dominant parcel.
(C) The utility rights were extinguished upon sale of the underlying property because licenses do not run with the land.
(D) The utility company holds a non-possessory license that may be terminated at will by any of the new residential grantees.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 公用事業機構（如電力、自來水、瓦斯公司）跨越私人土地架設管線所取得的權益屬於 個人地役權 / 無需役地地役權（Easement in Gross）。此類地役權僅有受物權負擔之「供役地（Servient Tenement）」，並無相鄰獲益之「需役地（Dominant Tenement）」，權利專屬於公用事業實體，在土地分割過戶後仍持續附隨負擔該土地。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 該權益並無獲益之需役地，非附屬地役權（Appurtenant easement）。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 已登記之商業個人地役權為實質物權，非隨意撤銷之許可（License），不因土地轉售而消滅。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 地役權非許可權，新地主無權任意將其終止。
Question 28
For seven continuous years, a homeowner openly, notoriously, and without the adjacent landholder's permission, utilized a gravel driveway crossing twelve feet over the neighbor's boundary line to access a detached workshop. The true landowner was fully aware of the daily vehicular usage but never objected, granted permission, or attempted to block access. Under California statutory prescription standards (requiring 5 years of open, notorious, continuous, and hostile use), what legal interest has the homeowner established?
連續七年，屋主公開、周知且未經鄰地所有權人許可，使用跨越鄰地界線十二英尺的碎石車道以通往獨立工坊。真正地主完全知悉每日車輛使用，但從未異議、授權或阻擋。依加州時效取得法定標準（須公開、周知、持續且敵對使用滿五年），該屋主已成立何種法律權益？
(A) A fee simple determinable estate over the twelve-foot gravel strip through adverse possession without property tax payments.
(B) A prescriptive easement across the driveway, having satisfied all statutory elements of open, notorious, hostile, and continuous use.
(C) A revocable neighborly license that terminates automatically upon recording a grant deed.
(D) An encroachment that immediately forfeits the workshop improvements to the true landowner.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 依加州法律，未經地主許可在他人土地上以「公開（Open）、公然顯著（Notorious）、對抗/敵對（Hostile/Adverse）且連續（Continuous）」之方式通行使用達法定 5 年以上者，即依法取得 時效地役權（Prescriptive Easement）。地主知情但未予正式授權亦未阻止，符合敵對未獲准要件。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 逆權侵占（Adverse Possession）取得土地「所有權（Fee title）」在加州必須連續 5 年「全額代繳該地塊之地產稅（Property taxes）」；時效地役權僅取得「使用權」，無須繳納地產稅。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 地主從未明示授與許可，不屬於許可權（License）。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 既已成就時效地役權，地主無權沒收工作坊建物。
Question 29
A property owner conveyed title to the westerly half of a large rural acreage while retaining the easterly half containing the owner's primary residence. Because the public highway was situated entirely on the western border, the conveyance left the retained eastern homestead completely landlocked with zero ingress and egress to any public thoroughfare. The deed contained no express reservation of access. Under common law real property principles, what legal easement is created by operation of law?
所有權人讓與大片鄉村土地的西半部，保留含主要住宅的東半部。因公路完全位於西界，此次讓與使保留的東部家園完全無出路，對任何公共道路皆無進出。契據未明示保留通行權。依普通法不動產原則，依法當然成立何種地役權？
(A) An easement in gross benefiting the general traveling public.
(B) A prescriptive easement requiring ten years of ongoing hostile trespassing before vesting.
(C) An easement by necessity, created by operation of law to prevent the retained parcel from remaining landlocked.
(D) An easement by dedication conveying complete fee title to the county transit authority.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 當地主分割土地導致其中一筆土地完全處於無路可通之「閉鎖狀態（Landlocked）」而無法進出公共道路時，基於公共政策不容許土地喪失利用價值之原則，法院將依法推定成立 必要地役權（Easement by Necessity）。該地役權僅在閉鎖必要性存續期間有效。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 此為特定相鄰地塊之通行權利，非供公眾使用之個人地役權。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 必要地役權隨土地分割閉鎖立即由法律創設，無須歷經多年敵對使用。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 奉獻地役權（Easement by dedication）係私地捐作公共道路使用，非私人通行權。
Question 30
Regarding the legal differences between an Express Grant, an Express Reservation, an Implied Easement, and an Easement by Prescription, all of the following legal assertions are true, EXCEPT:
關於明示授與、明示保留、默示地役權與時效地役權的法律差異，下列法律主張皆正確，惟除了：
(A) An express grant occurs when a servient tenement owner formally conveys an easement right to the dominant tenement owner in writing.
(B) An express reservation occurs when a grantor conveys real estate but expressly retains an easement over the conveyed parcel for the benefit of retained land.
(C) An implied easement requires proof that the prior unity of title existed and that the disputed use was apparent, permanent, and continuous at the time of the grant.
(D) An express verbal easement granted without consideration creates an irrevocable interest in land that permanently binds all subsequent grantees.
【正確答案】 (D)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (D) 解析： 依據反詐欺法（Statute of Frauds），明示地役權（Express Easement）屬於實質不動產物權，必須具備書面形式（Must be in writing）。任何「口頭（Verbal）」給予的使用特權在法律上僅屬 特許權/許可（License），可隨時撤銷，絕不構成永久拘束後續買家的不可撤銷地役權。選項 (D) 敘述錯誤，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 供役地地主以書面將通行權授予需役地地主，即為 明示授與（Express Grant）。
干擾選項 (B) 正確： 賣方售出土地時在契據中為自己保留通行權，即為 明示保留（Express Reservation）。
干擾選項 (C) 正確： 默示地役權（Implied Easement） 之成立要件包括原先產權同一（Unity of title）、讓與時使用外觀顯著（Apparent）、永久且連續合理必要。

California Real Estate Salesperson Examination: Master Practice Set 2 (Pages 5–20)
Topics Covered: Legal Licenses vs. Easements, The -OR/-EE Actor Rules, Bundle of Rights & Liens, Real vs. Personal Property Transformations, Fixture Disputes & MARIA Standards, Trade Fixtures in Commercial Leases, Appurtenances & Deeds, Boundary Encroachments & Surveys, Doctrine of Emblements, Surface & Subsurface Water Doctrines, Freehold Estates (Fee Simple & Life Estates), Reversions & Remaindermen, Leasehold Classifications (Years, Periodic, Sufferance, Will), Lease Types (Gross, Net, Percentage, Lease Option), Property Management Agency & Risk Control, and Eviction Principles.
Question 1
An owner of an upscale residential property verbally agreed to allow a neighbor to park a luxury motorhome on the paved rear driveway for four months without demanding any monetary compensation; however, three weeks later, after accepting a formal purchase offer from a third party, the owner revoked permission and demanded the vehicle’s immediate removal within twenty-four hours. Regarding the legal status and enforceability of the neighbor's interest, which of the following statements is TRUE?
高檔住宅所有權人口頭同意讓鄰居將豪華旅居車停放於鋪設後車道四個月，且不要求任何金錢補償；然而三週後，在接受第三人正式購買要約後，所有權人撤回許可，並要求車輛於二十四小時內立刻移走。關於鄰居權益的法律地位與可執行性，下列敘述何者正確？
(A) The oral agreement created an irrevocable appurtenant easement running with the land.
(B) The arrangement constituted a revocable personal license creating no interest in the realty.
(C) The neighbor acquired an estate at will requiring a thirty-day advance written notice to quit.
(D) The agreement established an executed negative easement enforceable against subsequent buyers.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 許可權（License）並非不動產的物權或產權利益（Not an estate or interest in land），僅屬於進入並使用他人土地的「個人特權（Personal privilege）」。許可權可由口頭合約成立、通常不具對價，地主得隨時單方予以撤銷（Freely revocable），且不具附隨土地移轉之效力（Does not run with the land）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 附屬地役權（Appurtenant easement）屬於實質物權負擔，依反詐欺法（Statute of Frauds）必須具備書面形式，無法僅憑無償口頭約定成立。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 隨意租賃（Estate at will）屬於租賃權（Leasehold estate），需要具備租賃合意與排他佔有權限，口頭停車特權僅屬單純特許使用。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 消極地役權（Negative easement，如採光或景觀地役權）同樣必須為書面物權設定，口頭許可無法轉化為拘束後續買家的地役權。
Question 2
In standard real estate contract drafting and secured lending instruments governed by the statutory "-OR" and "-EE" rule, the designated suffix identifies the party who executes or grants an interest versus the party who receives or accepts it. In this legal context, which of the following pairings is INCORRECT?
在受法定「-OR」與「-EE」規則規範的標準不動產契約草擬與擔保放貸文件中，該字尾用以識別執行或授與權益的一方，相對接受或收受權益的一方。在此法律語境下，下列哪一組配對是錯誤的？
(A) Optionor — The prospective buyer receiving the unilateral right to acquire title.
(B) Mortgagor — The property owner pledging real estate collateral to secure financing.
(C) Trustor — The borrower conveying legal title to a trustee under a deed of trust.
(D) Grantor — The owner executing a formal instrument of conveyance to pass title.
【正確答案】 (A)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (A) 解析： 在選擇權契約（Option Contract）中，給予購買權利的屋主/賣方是「給予者」，字尾為 -OR，稱為 Optionor（選擇權授與人）；接受購買權利的潛在買方是「接受者」，字尾為 -EE，稱為 Optionee（選擇權受領人）。選項 (A) 將 Optionor 定義為買方，配對錯誤，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (B) 正確： 抵押人（Mortgagor）是借款人，將房產抵押擔保「給予/設定」給放款銀行（Mortgagee）。
干擾選項 (C) 正確： 信託委託人（Trustor）是借款人，將產權信託轉移給受託人（Trustee）以擔保債務。
干擾選項 (D) 正確： 讓與人（Grantor）是簽署契據將產權移轉給受讓人（Grantee）的原屋主。
Question 3
While fee simple real property ownership is legally characterized as an expansive "bundle of rights" granting the holder comprehensive authority over possession, disposition, and control, these sticks are subject to external encumbrances and sovereign powers. With respect to the legal limitations and characteristics of this bundle, all of the following statements are true, EXCEPT:
完全產權在法律上被描述為賦予持有人對佔有、處分與控制廣泛權限的「權利束」，但這些權杖仍受外部負擔與主權公權力拘束。關於此權利束的法律限制與特徵，下列敘述皆正確，惟除了：
(A) The bundle of rights includes the affirmative legal privilege to refuse to exercise any single right.
(B) Fee simple absolute ownership grants permanent immunity from local municipal zoning and police power.
(C) The recording of a mechanic's lien temporarily suspends specific disposition rights within the bundle.
(D) An encumbrance voluntarily granted by the owner diminishes the unencumbered market value of the estate.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 產權所有人的權利束（Bundle of Rights）恆受政府四大公權力（PETE：Police Power 警察權、Eminent Domain 徵收權、Taxation 課稅權、Escheat 充公權）的限制，絕不存在免除政府警察權或分區管制的「絕對豁免權」。選項 (B) 敘述錯誤，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 權利束不僅包含行使各項權益（使用、出租、處分等）的權限，亦包含「拒絕行使（Refuse to exercise）」該等權利的自由。
干擾選項 (C) 正確： 技工留置權（Mechanic's lien）屬於法定物權負擔，在清償註銷前會限制產權處分與乾淨產權的轉讓。
干擾選項 (D) 正確： 自願設定之抵押或地役權等負擔，會使未受負擔之產權淨值相應減少。
Question 4
Prior to the execution of a residential grant deed, the seller signed an unrecorded contract agreeing to sell fifty mature cedar trees situated on the estate to a commercial timber company, allowing the company thirty days after closing to cut and harvest the lumber. Simultaneously, the seller stacked three hundred uninstalled marble tiles in the garage to renovate the master bathroom. Regarding the legal classification of these items upon conveyance of the parcel, which of the following is CORRECT?
在簽署住宅授與契據前，賣方簽署未經登記的契約，同意將莊園上五十棵成熟雪松售予商業木材公司，允許該公司於交屋後三十日內砍伐收穫木材。同時，賣方將三百片未安裝的大理石磁磚堆放於車庫，準備翻修主臥浴室。關於讓與該地時這些物品的法律定性，下列何者正確？
(A) The standing timber converted immediately into personal property under the doctrine of constructive severance.
(B) The unlaid marble tiles automatically became fixtures under the principle of custom architectural adaptation.
(C) The standing trees remain real property until physically severed, while the uninstalled tiles remain personal property.
(D) Both the standing cedar trees and the unlaid marble tiles are classified legally as fructus industriales.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 未經砍伐之天然樹木為土地附著物，在實體砍伐分離（Severance）前仍屬不動產（Real Property）；堆放於車庫、尚未鋪設水泥安裝的石磚，因欠缺實質附著（Annexation）與整合，仍屬動產（Personal Property / Chattels）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 樹木在未實體砍伐分離前，對善意買方而言物權本質仍為不動產。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 散落未安裝建材不符合定著物判定標準（MARIA）。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 天然樹木屬於天然產物（Fructus naturales），人工定期耕作之農作物才屬於勞動產物（Fructus industriales / Emblements）。
Question 5
A bitter legal dispute arose between the buyer and the seller of an executive residence regarding an ornate, custom-fitted wrought-iron chandelier suspended from the foyer ceiling by reinforced anchor bolts and hardwired into the home’s primary electrical circuit. If the purchase agreement remained completely silent regarding lighting fixtures, which legal test within the MARIA framework will the court deem the MOST determinative?
行政宅邸的買方與賣方就一盞華麗、客製安裝的鍛鐵吊燈發生激烈爭議；該燈以強化錨栓懸掛於門廳天花板，並硬接至住宅主電路。若買賣契約對燈具完全未作約定，法院依 MARIA 架構將以哪一項法律測試最具決定性？
(A) The adaptability of the article to the specialized architectural layout of the entryway.
(B) The objective intention of the party who originally installed and affixed the item.
(C) The high historical acquisition cost and verified antique appraisal value of the article.
(D) The specific relationship of the parties favoring the seller over the prospective buyer.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 在判定定著物的 MARIA 測試法中，當書面買賣契約（Agreement）未作明文約定時，法院最核心且具決定性的法律標準為「安裝附著時的客觀意圖（Intention of the annexor）」，並輔以附著方式（Method）與專用適應性（Adaptability）來佐證該意圖。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 適應性（Adaptability）雖為測試要素之一，但主要用於佐證當初安裝者的客觀永久留置意圖。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 物品的「原始購置成本」完全不是 MARIA 測試法的法定判斷標準。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 在當事人關係（Relationship of the parties）的法律推定中，法院在買賣爭議中通常「偏向保護買方（Favor buyer over seller）」。
Question 6
A commercial tenant operating a high-end specialty bakery leased a retail storefront for a five-year term and subsequently anchored commercial convection ovens to the structural flooring and connected industrial ventilation hoods to the roof ducts. Two weeks prior to lease termination, the tenant began detaching these units. The landlord obtained a temporary restraining order, asserting the equipment converted into real property through permanent physical annexation. Which of the following legal determinations is CORRECT?
經營高檔特色烘焙的商業租戶承租零售店面五年，隨後將商用對流烤箱錨固於結構地板，並將工業排氣罩接至屋頂風管。租期終止前兩週，租戶開始拆除這些設備。房東取得暫時禁制令，主張該設備已因永久實體附著轉為不動產。下列法律判斷何者正確？
(A) The commercial ovens and ventilation hoods became permanent fixtures that belong exclusively to the landlord.
(B) The equipment items are trade fixtures classified as personal property, which the tenant may remove provided all damage is repaired.
(C) The baking equipment converted to appurtenances running with the commercial title under the doctrine of accession.
(D) The tenant may remove the ovens only if the landlord provides prior written consent and tenders salvage compensation.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 商業租戶為經營業務所需而安裝的設備屬於「營業用定著物（Trade Fixtures）」。在法律上，營業定著物仍保留為承租人的「動產（Personal property）」，承租人有權在租期屆滿前將其拆除移走，但負有修復因拆除造成之實體損害（Restore damages）的法定義務。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 營業用設備不因鎖入地板而喪失動產屬性，並未自動撥歸房東所有。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 從屬物（Appurtenance）是隨土地移轉之附屬權益或建物，並非商業租客之營業設備。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 租客拆除自身合法動產設備為法定權利，無須取得房東同意或補償。
Question 7
A rural property owner transferred fee title of a 40-acre agricultural parcel to a buyer via a standard grant deed. Situated on the property was an old detached wooden granary, a recorded access road easement across the adjacent westerly tract, and subterranean mineral rights. The deed contained no explicit mention of the granary, the westerly access easement, or the subsurface mineral rights. Following the closing of escrow, which of the following statements accurately reflects the legal status of these items?
鄉村地主以標準授與契據將四十英畝農地的完全產權移轉給買方。該地有一棟獨立舊木穀倉、跨越西側鄰地已登記之通行地役權，以及地下礦物權。契據未明示提及穀倉、西側通行地役權或地下礦物權。履約保證完成後，下列何者正確反映這些項目的法律地位？
(A) The easement, granary, and mineral rights automatically passed to the buyer as appurtenances running with the land.
(B) The grantor retained exclusive subsurface mineral rights under the presumption that all subterranean rights require express deeds.
(C) The access road easement was extinguished by merger because it was not specifically re-granted within the deed text.
(D) The detached granary remained the personal property of the grantor because outbuildings are treated as personalty.
【正確答案】 (A)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (A) 解析： 從屬物與從屬權利（Appurtenance）包含附著於土地之建築物（如穀倉）、附屬地役權（Appurtenant easements）及地下礦物權利。從屬物具備「附隨土地移轉（Runs with the land）」之法律效力，即使契據（Deed）內文未逐一明載，亦隨主體土地所有權之移轉自動移轉給受讓人（Grantee）。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 除非讓與人在契據中明確設定「保留條款（Reservation）」，否則所有地下權利隨產權一併移轉。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 附屬地役權不會因契據未提及而消滅，混同（Merger）僅發生於需役地與供役地所有權歸同一人所有時。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 固定座落於土地之穀倉屬於不動產定著物/建築物，並非動產。
Question 8
A professional land survey completed during a pending residential sales transaction revealed that an adjoining homeowner had constructed a concrete driveway and brick retaining wall that physically intruded two feet across the property line onto the seller’s parcel. Under California real property law, how is this physical intrusion classified, and what is its legal nature?
在進行中的住宅買賣交易期間完成的專業土地測量顯示，鄰屋屋主興建的混凝土車道與磚擋土牆實體侵入賣方地界兩英尺。依加州不動產法，此種實體侵入如何定性，其法律性質為何？
(A) An appurtenant easement that automatically converted into an incorporeal hereditament upon discovery.
(B) An encroachment, which constitutes a physical intrusion upon the land and represents a form of continuous actionable trespass.
(C) An implied license that grants the neighbor permanent equitable servitude over the affected boundary strip.
(D) An emblement that entitles the encroaching neighbor to ongoing seasonal exploitation under the doctrine of estovers.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 建築物、改良物或圍牆車道未經授權跨越相鄰地界線之實體侵入，稱為「越界侵占（Encroachment）」。在法律性質上，越界侵占構成持續性的「非法侵入（Trespass）」，通常經由專業土地測量（Survey）發現，受害地主得請求法院核發禁制令排除或請求損害賠償。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 未經授權之實體越界並非合法創設的地役權（Easement）。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 越界侵占並非許可（License），亦非衡平地役負擔。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 收益動產（Emblements）專指年度人工耕作農作物，與建築構造物越界完全無關。
Question 9
A tenant farmer entered into a five-year agricultural lease to plant and harvest hybrid corn. In the second year of the lease term, the life tenant who leased the acreage suddenly died, instantly extinguishing the underlying leasehold estate two months prior to the scheduled harvest. The remainderman took immediate possession of the farm and barred the farmer from entering the fields, asserting full title to the standing unharvested corn. Under the common law doctrine of emblements, which of the following statements is CORRECT?
一位佃農簽訂五年農業租約種植並收穫雜交玉米。租期第二年，出租該農地的終身產權人突然過世，使租賃權立刻消滅，且距預定收成尚有兩個月。剩餘權人立即佔有農場並禁止農夫進入田地，主張未收割的玉米全歸其所有。依普通法農作物孳息原則（emblements），下列敘述何者正確？
(A) The standing crops converted to real property adhering to the remainderman’s estate upon lessor death.
(B) The tenant farmer retains the legal right to enter the parcel to cultivate and harvest that season’s annual crops as personal property.
(C) The farmer forfeited all rights to the corn because an agricultural lease automatically creates a tenancy at sufferance upon lessor death.
(D) The remainderman may confiscate the crops provided the estate reimburses the farmer for baseline seed costs.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 依據「農作物收益權法則（Doctrine of Emblements）」，由承租農民經由勞動人工耕種之年度農作物（Fructus Industriales）在法律上被視為農民的「動產（Personal property）」。若租約因不可預見之情事（如終身權人非因農民過錯而身故）提前終止，承租農民依法享有在該生長季結束前，重新進入土地照料並採收農作物的默示法定權利。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 年度人工農作物不因土地所有權回歸而轉化為地主的不動產。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 農民並未構成容忍租賃（Estate at sufferance），且 Emblements 法則正是為保障農民勞動成果而設。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 剩餘權人（Remainderman）無權沒收農作物，農民享有完整採收處分權。
Question 10
Regarding the legal doctrines governing water rights and the physical alterations caused by water movement across real property boundaries, which of the following statements is LEAST likely correct?
關於規範水權以及水流穿越不動產邊界所造成實體變更的法律原則，下列哪一項最不可能正確？
(A) Riparian rights govern water usage for property owners whose land borders flowing watercourses such as rivers and streams.
(B) Littoral rights govern water usage for landowners whose properties border non-flowing, navigable bodies of water such as lakes and oceans.
(C) Avulsion refers to the slow, imperceptible accumulation of soil upon a shoreline resulting in the gradual enlargement of the riparian parcel.
(D) Under the doctrine of correlative use, a landowner holds the reasonable right to extract subterranean percolating water for agricultural irrigation.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 水流緩慢、漸進沉積土壤使土地面積增加之現象稱為 沖積（Accretion）（沉積物稱為 Alluvium）；崩蝕（Avulsion） 則是指因暴洪、水壩決堤或地震等突發且劇烈的自然外力造成土地瞬間被撕裂流失之現象。選項 (C) 定義錯誤，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 河流/溪流等流動水體之水權稱為河流沿岸水權（Riparian Rights，記憶點：Riparian = River）。
干擾選項 (B) 正確： 湖泊/海洋等非流動水體之水權稱為湖泊海洋水權（Littoral Rights，記憶點：Littoral = Lake）。
干擾選項 (D) 正確： 關聯使用權（Correlative Use）允許地主在合理範圍內抽取地下水或河水進行灌溉。
Question 11
In an arid Western jurisdiction governed strictly by statutory water allocation rather than traditional common law riparian principles, a commercial cattle rancher purchased a parcel bordering a natural stream and immediately diverted seventy percent of the river’s volume for private mining operations, entirely depriving downstream agricultural farms established twenty years earlier. The downstream farmers filed for an injunction citing the Doctrine of Prior Appropriation. Under this statutory doctrine, how will the dispute be adjudicated?
在一處嚴格受法定水資源分配（而非傳統普通法河岸權原則）規範的乾旱西部司法管轄區，商業牧牛場主購入臨天然溪流的地塊，立刻截取河川流量七成供私人採礦，使二十年前即已設立的下游農場合然無水。下游農夫依「先佔用水原則」聲請禁制令。依該法定原則，爭議將如何裁決？
(A) The commercial rancher prevails because physical adjacency to flowing water grants paramount riparian entitlements over non-adjacent properties.
(B) The downstream farmers prevail because water rights are allocated chronologically based on the historical date of beneficial use established by state permit.
(C) The dispute is settled by dividing the water volume equally between all adjoining landowners under correlative apportionment.
(D) The rancher prevails provided the water diversion does not reduce the navigable depth required for commercial maritime transit.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 在乾旱缺水地區適用的「先佔先得水權原則（Doctrine of Prior Appropriation）」，水權之分配並非取決於土地是否與水體相鄰（Geographic adjacency），而是由州政府依據申請取得許可並投入「有益用途（Beneficial use）」的「時間先後順序（Chronological priority）」來決定（即 First in time, first in right）。下游農民建立有益使用在先，享有優先上位水權。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 在 Prior Appropriation 管轄下，傳統河流沿岸相鄰權（Riparian rights）已被法規優先原則取代。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 關聯均分原則（Correlative use）適用於地下水等共同合理使用，非 Prior Appropriation 爭端裁決方式。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 航行權非先佔水權之核心判定基準，上游無權剝奪既有核准之有益用水配額。
Question 12
A grantor executed a fee simple deed conveying a historic parcel to a municipal school district stating: "To the City School District so long as the property is used exclusively for educational public instruction; but if ever used for commercial warehousing, the title shall instantly revert to the grantor, his heirs, or assigns." Five years later, the school board leased the property to a private logistics firm for freight staging. What specific estate was conveyed, and what is the current legal status of the title?
讓與人以完全產權契據將一歷史地塊讓與市立學區，載明：「予市立學區，惟該不動產須專供公共教育教學使用；若改作商業倉儲，產權即刻回歸讓與人、其繼承人或不特定受讓人。」五年後，教育委員會將該地出租予私人物流公司作為貨運調度場。當初讓與的是何種產權，目前產權法律狀態為何？
(A) Fee simple absolute; the district retains unrestricted fee title because deed restraints against commercial leases are void.
(B) Fee simple subject to a condition subsequent; the grantor must affirmatively file a quiet title lawsuit to exercise the right of entry.
(C) Fee simple determinable; title automatically reverted to the original grantor immediately upon the breach of the limitation.
(D) Life estate pur autre vie; title vested in the municipal board for the measuring life of the city mayor.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 契據中使用「只要...（So long as / While / During）」等限定詞，且明定違反時產權「立即自動回歸（Automatically revert）」者，屬於 限定決定可廢止產權（Fee Simple Determinable）。一旦指定事件或限制條件遭到違反，所有權無須經由司法訴訟或讓與人主張進入，即在法律上「自動終止並回歸」原讓與人或其繼承人。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 該契據附帶明確限制條件，非完全無條件所有權（Fee simple absolute）。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 解除條件產權（Fee simple subject to a condition subsequent）使用 "provided that / on condition that"，且違反應由讓與人主動採取行動行使重新進入權（Right of re-entry），不會自動復權。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 題幹並非以某人之生命存續期為限，非終身產權（Life estate）。
Question 13
An elderly homeowner executed a valid grant deed conveying a residential property to his adult son, "reserving unto the father a Life Estate for the duration of the father's natural life, with the remainder vesting in fee simple in the grantor’s daughter upon the father's death." During his occupancy, the father mortgaged the property with a private lender and entered into a written four-year lease with a residential tenant. Which of the following legal outcomes is CORRECT regarding these encumbrances?
年長屋主以有效授與契據將住宅產權讓與成年兒子，「為父親保留其有生之年的終身產權，父親過世後剩餘權以完全產權歸屬於讓與人之女兒。」佔有期間，父親以該屋向私人放貸人設定抵押，並與住宅租戶簽訂四年書面租約。關於這些負擔，下列法律結果何者正確？
(A) The private mortgage lien and the residential lease automatically terminate the moment the father dies.
(B) The daughter takes fee title upon the father's death subject to the mandatory fulfillment of the remaining lease term.
(C) The daughter becomes personally liable for the outstanding mortgage balance executed by her late father under remainder accession.
(D) The conveyance of the life estate is void ab initio because life tenants lack the legal capacity to execute leases or mortgages.
【正確答案】 (A)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (A) 解析： 終身權人（Life Tenant）在自身生命存續期間（Measuring life）享有佔有、出租或抵押物業之權限；然而，其所能處分之權益上限不得超越其自身終身權限。因此，終身權人簽訂之任何租約或抵押設定，於其「身故之瞬間自動隨之消滅（Automatically terminate upon death）」，不得拘束後續取得完全產權之剩餘權人（Remainderman）。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 剩餘權人（女兒）取得完全產權時不受原租約拘束，租約已隨生命計量人之身故終止。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 女兒未簽署借據，亦非抵押主體，不承擔任何個人清償責任，抵押權隨終身產權一同消滅。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 終身權人具備完全行為能力得在其存續期內簽署租賃與抵押合約。
Question 14
A life tenant holding a residential life estate pur autre vie measured by the life of an ailing uncle deliberately neglected property maintenance, failed to pay municipal property taxes for three consecutive years, and began felling a protected grove of heritage oak trees to sell as commercial firewood. Which legal remedy is the remainderman entitled to pursue against the life tenant?
持有以生病叔父之生命為衡量的終身產權（pur autre vie）之人，故意疏於維護、連續三年未繳市政地產稅，並砍伐受保護的古櫟樹林作為商業柴薪出售。剩餘權人得對終身產權人主張何種法律救濟？
(A) File an action for constructive eviction to declare the life estate converted into a periodic tenancy.
(B) File a legal action for waste, seeking monetary damages and an injunction to halt the physical and economic destruction of the reversionary interest.
(C) Demand immediate acceleration of the underlying fee simple promissory note under the alienation clause.
(D) Petition the local zoning board to execute an inverse condemnation against the life tenant.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 終身權人對物業負有繳納日常稅捐、維持屋況之法定義務，且「嚴禁造成損耗/毀損（Prohibited from committing waste）」。砍伐保護林木、蓄意荒廢物業及欠稅損及物業價值與未來剩餘權人之權益，構成法律上的 毀損物業（Waste），剩餘權人有權向法院起訴請求損害賠償並聲請禁制令（Injunctive relief）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 推定驅逐（Constructive eviction）是租賃法中租客對抗房東違法不作為之救濟手段。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 讓與條款（Alienation clause）為借款融資合約條款，與終身權人的毀損責任無關。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 反向徵收（Inverse condemnation）是私有地主向政府索賠之訴訟，非私人侵權救濟。
Question 15
Regarding the fundamental legal distinctions between Freehold Estates and Less Than Freehold (Leasehold) Estates, all of the following comparisons are accurate, EXCEPT:
關於自由保有產權（Freehold Estates）與低於自由保有之租賃產權（Leasehold Estates）的根本法律區別，下列比較皆正確，惟除了：
(A) Freehold estates are characterized by an ownership interest of indefinite or unpredictable duration.
(B) Less than freehold estates represent possessory interests held for a definite, fixed, or determinable period of time.
(C) Both freehold estates and leasehold estates are legally classified under California civil law as real property chattels.
(D) The holder of a fee simple estate possesses the underlying title, whereas a leasehold tenant holds personal property rights termed chattels real.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 自由繼承產權（Freehold estates）在法律上屬於實質的「不動產所有權（Real Property）」；而租賃權（Leasehold / Less than freehold estates）本質上屬於動產性質的「動產不動產化權益 / 不動產動產（Chattels real / Personal property）」。將兩者皆歸類為動產是完全錯誤的，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 自由產權（如 Fee simple 或 Life estate）之存續期間均為不確定或不可預期之期間（Indefinite duration）。
干擾選項 (B) 正確： 非自由產權（租賃權）之佔有使用具有確定或可判定之期限。
干擾選項 (D) 正確： 完全產權人持有 Real Property 產權，而租客持有的是租賃動產權益（Chattels real）。
Question 16
A tenant entered into a written lease for a commercial warehouse beginning on June 1, 2026, and explicitly terminating at midnight on November 30, 2026. On November 15, 2026, the landlord demanded that the tenant vacate immediately, asserting that because the landlord had not served a thirty-day notice to quit, the lease converted into a month-to-month tenancy. The tenant refused to vacate prior to November 30. Under landlord-tenant law, how is this leasehold classified, and what notice was legally required to end it on November 30?
租戶簽訂商業倉庫書面租約，自 2026 年 6 月 1 日起至 2026 年 11 月 30 日午夜明示終止。2026 年 11 月 15 日，房東要求租戶立刻遷出，主張因未送達三十日終止通知，租約已轉為按月租賃。租戶拒絕於 11 月 30 日前遷出。依房東租戶法，此租賃權如何定性，於 11 月 30 日終止依法須為何種通知？
(A) Periodic tenancy; requiring forty-five days advance written statutory notice from either party.
(B) Tenancy at sufferance; requiring judicial process and a three-day notice to pay or quit.
(C) Estate for years; terminating automatically upon the arrival of the agreed expiration date without any advance notice.
(D) Estate at will; requiring reasonable verbal notice given the commercial character of the premises.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 凡具有「確定開始與結束日期（Definite start and end date）」的租賃合約，不論期間長短（數月或數年），皆屬於 定期租賃（Estate for Years / Tenancy for Years）。定期租賃於約定期滿日「自動終止（Self-terminates）」，無須任何一方發出提前解約通知（No notice required）。租客在 11 月 30 日前享有合法佔有權。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 週期性租賃（Periodic tenancy）無固定結束日，按期自動滾動，才需要法定提前通知。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 容忍租賃（Tenancy at sufferance）發生在租約期滿後租客賴著不走之狀態，租期內不構成。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 隨意租賃（Estate at will）存續期不固定，本案有明確約定起訖日。
Question 17
A residential tenant leased an apartment under a written month-to-month rental agreement. After continuously occupying the premises and paying rent on the first of every month for two years, the tenant decided to relocate to another city. Regarding the legal requirements to effectively terminate this periodic tenancy, which of the following is CORRECT?
住宅租戶依書面按月租約承租公寓。連續佔有並於每月一日繳租兩年後，租戶決定遷往他市。關於有效終止此定期租賃的法律要件，下列何者正確？
(A) The lease terminates automatically at the conclusion of the calendar month without any notice requirements.
(B) The tenant must provide proper statutory advance written notice to the landlord prior to terminating the periodic tenancy.
(C) The tenant may terminate immediately without notice provided the security deposit is surrendered as liquidated damages.
(D) The death of either the landlord or the tenant immediately and automatically extinguishes the periodic tenancy by operation of law.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 按月續約之 週期性租賃（Periodic Tenancy） 在法律上會自動連續遞延進入下一個週期，若任一方欲終止租約，必須依法提前給予書面解約通知（Statutory advance written notice）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 期滿自動終止且不需通知的是定期租賃（Estate for years），而非週期性租賃。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 押金（Security deposit）依法不可作為任意免除法定解約通知義務的違約金。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 週期性租賃不因房東或房客之死亡而自動終止，其租賃權益與義務轉由遺產繼承人/遺產管理人承接。
Question 18
A tenant whose one-year written residential lease expired on December 31 continued to occupy the premises throughout January without the landlord's consent and without remitting any rental payments. The landlord refused to accept further funds and initiated legal proceedings. During the period of unauthorized holdover occupancy prior to court eviction, what legal estate did the tenant hold?
租戶一年期書面住宅租約於 12 月 31 日屆滿後，未經房東同意且未繳任何租金，於整個一月繼續佔用。房東拒絕接受後續款項並提起法律程序。在法院驅逐前的無權滯留期間，租戶持有何種法律產權？
(A) Estate at will, entitling the occupant to undisturbed possession until an equitable accounting is rendered.
(B) Estate at sufferance, resulting from the tenant wrongfully retaining possession after the expiration of lawful tenancy.
(C) Unlawful trespass ab initio, subjecting the occupant to immediate summary criminal arrest without civil process.
(D) Executory life tenancy, permitting the tenant to remain until the landlord tenders formal compensation.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 當承租人於合法租約期滿後，未獲房東同意且未繳租金仍繼續非法滯留佔有物業時，該滯留租客（Holdover tenant）所持有之法律地位稱為 容忍租賃 / 轉非法人住租賃（Estate at Sufferance / Tenancy at Sufferance）。因其最初進駐時具合法權源，故非自始擅闖（Not trespass ab initio），但房東必須循民事非法居留訴訟程序（Unlawful detainer）進行驅逐，不得採取自力救濟。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 隨意租賃（Estate at will）必須建立在房東允許或合意佔有的基礎上。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 由於最初為合法進駐，民法上不構成自始刑事擅闖，警察不得在無法院驅逐令下直接刑事逮捕。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 超期滯留完全不產生終身產權或補償請求權。
Question 19
During a severe winter cold snap, the centralized heating boiler in a residential multi-family building malfunctioned. Despite four successive written notices served by the tenant over a twenty-five-day period, the landlord deliberately refused to hire a licensed technician, leaving the unit uninhabitable with indoor temperatures plunging below freezing. Unable to endure the conditions, the tenant vacated the premises entirely and surrendered the keys. Under California property jurisprudence, which legal doctrine did the tenant execute?
嚴冬寒流期間，多戶住宅大樓中央暖氣鍋爐故障。租戶於二十五日內連續送達四次書面通知，房東仍故意拒絕聘請合格技師，使室內氣溫降至冰點以下而不適居住。租戶無法忍受，完全遷出並交還鑰匙。依加州財產法理，租戶行使的是何種法律原則？
(A) Actual physical ejectment under the doctrine of eminent forfeiture.
(B) Constructive eviction, discharging the tenant from any ongoing or future rent payment liabilities.
(C) Tenancy at sufferance with an offset claim for retaliatory waste.
(D) Implied quiet title action against the landlord's reversionary estate.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 當房東嚴重違反維持物業適合人居之法定義務（如寒冬拒不修繕暖氣），導致房屋實質上無法居住，迫使租客不得不搬離並交還管轄權時，法律上構成 推定驅逐 / 實質驅逐（Constructive Eviction）。租客完成實質搬遷（Vacate）後，依法徹底免除後續所有租金支付義務，並得向房東求償。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 實體驅逐（Actual eviction）是指房東透過執法機關實體驅離租客。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 租客主動撤出係行使合法解約抗辯，並非容忍租賃。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 租客並非主張物業所有權，與確認產權之訴（Quiet title）無關。
Question 20
A commercial retail tenant holding a five-year lease negotiated a transaction whereby a third party assumed the entire unexpired four-year balance of the lease term, taking full possession of the retail unit and paying rent directly to the property owner. The landlord did not execute a novation or release. Under contract and real property law, what transaction occurred, and what is the original tenant's liability?
持有五年租約的商業零售租戶洽談一筆交易，由第三人承擔剩餘四年租期、完全佔有該店面並直接向業主繳租。房東未簽署契約更新（novation）或免除。依契約與不動產法，發生了何種交易，原租戶的責任為何？
(A) A sublease occurred; the original tenant remains primarily and solely liable to the landlord for all rent obligations.
(B) An assignment occurred; the assignee assumes primary liability, but the original tenant remains secondarily liable.
(C) A novation occurred by implication; completely discharging the original tenant from all contractual obligations.
(D) An accord and satisfaction occurred; converting the leasehold into a unilateral license.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 將租賃合約中「所有剩餘期限與空間」完整移轉給第三人之法律行為稱為 合約轉讓（Assignment）。在轉讓後，受讓人（Assignee）對房東負主要履行責任（基於物權關係 Privity of estate），但原承租人（Assignor）基於原契約關係（Privity of contract）仍負有次要連帶保證責任（Secondarily liable），除非房東簽署新約取代（Novation）明確免除其責任。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 分租/次租（Sublease）僅移轉部分空間或部分租期，原租客對房東維持第一線主要責任。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 契約更新（Novation）必須有三方明確合意簽署，不得僅憑默示推定免責。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 和解與清償（Accord and satisfaction）為債務爭議之解決方式，與租約轉讓無關。
Question 21
Regarding the implied or express covenant of quiet enjoyment embedded in California residential and commercial leases, which of the following statements is TRUE?
關於嵌入加州住宅與商業租約中之默示或明示安靜享用契約（covenant of quiet enjoyment），下列敘述何者正確？
(A) The covenant guarantees that neighboring property owners will never create exterior noise exceeding municipal decibel thresholds.
(B) The covenant protects the tenant against wrongful physical interference or disturbance of possession by the landlord or anyone holding superior title.
(C) The covenant allows the landlord the unrestricted legal right to enter the leased premises at any hour without notice to conduct random inspections.
(D) The covenant guarantees that the tenant's commercial business operations will generate profitable financial returns during the lease term.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 安寧享有權條款（Covenant of Quiet Enjoyment） 是租賃契約的核心法定保證，確保承租人在租賃期間享有和平佔有與使用物業之權益，免受來自房東本人、其代理人或任何持有上位合法產權者（Superior title）之非法實體干擾、侵入或妨礙。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 安寧享有權並非保證社區外部完全無噪音，而是針對產權干擾與房東行為之法律保障。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 房東任意無故侵入租客居所恰恰構成對安寧享有權的實質侵犯（Breach of quiet enjoyment）。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 法律不對租客之商業獲利提供任何擔保。
Question 22
A licensed real estate broker entered into a formal written property management agreement with an absentee owner to manage a 40-unit residential apartment complex. In this capacity, which of the following correctly classifies the broker’s agency status and primary legal objective?
持照不動產經紀人與不在場業主簽訂正式書面物業管理契約，管理一棟四十戶住宅公寓。在此身分下，下列何者正確界定經紀人之代理地位與主要法律目標？
(A) Special agent; whose sole legal objective is ensuring zero residential tenant vacancies at below-market rates.
(B) Universal agent; whose objective is liquidating real property assets to achieve immediate capital appreciation.
(C) General agent; whose primary objective is maximizing net operating income while preserving and maintaining property asset value.
(D) Dual agent; whose primary fiduciary duty is representing both the property owner and the individual tenants equally.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 物業經理人（Property Manager）獲授權持續處理特定物業的日常營運與管理事務，在代理法上屬於 一般代理人（General Agent）。物業經理對屋主負有完整的受託人信託義務（Fiduciary duties），其核心營運目標為：在維持並提升物業資產價值（Preserving property value）的同時，創造最高之物業淨營運收入（Maximizing net operating income）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 房屋買賣委託經紀人為特別代理人（Special agent）；低價換取零空置率違反最大化收益目標。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 全權代理人（Universal agent）具備無限處分權（如全權委託書 POA），非一般物管合約之定位。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 物業經理係受僱於屋主，信託忠誠責任專屬於屋主，非雙重代理。
Question 23
A comprehensive risk management audit performed by a professional property manager for a multi-tenant commercial office building resulted in several operational directives. When matching these operational decisions with categorized risk management principles, which of the following pairings is INCORRECT?
專業物業管理人對多租戶商業辦公大樓進行全面風險管理稽核後，作成若干營運指示。將這些營運決定與風險管理原則配對時，下列哪一組配對是錯誤的？
(A) Installing commercial fire sprinkler systems and electronic smoke detection alarms $\rightarrow$ Risk Control.
(B) Removing a deteriorating wooden pedestrian footbridge and closing off an unsafe rooftop deck $\rightarrow$ Risk Avoidance.
(C) Purchasing an all-risk commercial hazard insurance policy carrying a substantial $25,000 deductible $\rightarrow$ Risk Retention.
(D) Purchasing a comprehensive third-party liability insurance policy with zero deductible $\rightarrow$ Risk Severance.
【正確答案】 (D)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (D) 解析： 風險管理四大策略包含：規避（Avoidance）、自留（Retention/Dealing）、控制（Control）與轉嫁（Transfer）。購買全面保險將潛在賠償責任全數移轉給保險公司承擔，屬於 風險轉嫁（Risk Transfer）；不動產法中並無所謂「風險分離（Risk Severance）」之術語（Severance 係指將不動產分離為動產之物權行為）。配對錯誤，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 安裝滅火灑水與警報器以降低災害損害程度，屬於 風險控制（Risk Control）。
干擾選項 (B) 正確： 拆除危險設施以徹底消滅危險源，屬於 風險規避（Risk Avoidance）。
干擾選項 (C) 正確： 選擇高自負額保單自行吸收小額損失，屬於 風險自留/承擔（Risk Retention）。
Question 24
Under a standard residential lease agreement where the written contract remains completely silent regarding the specific calendar due date for rental payments, when is the rent legally due and payable by the tenant under California law?
在標準住宅租約中，書面契約對租金具體到期日完全未作約定時，依加州法律，租金依法於何時到期應付？
(A) On the first business day of the designated leasing period in advance.
(B) On the fifteenth day of each calendar month under the doctrine of substantial performance.
(C) On the final calendar day of the designated leasing period.
(D) Whenever the landlord tenders a formal written demand for accounting.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 若租賃契約中未明確約定租金給付期日（Contract is silent on due date），依據普通法與加州法定租賃推定原則，租金並非預先在月初支付，而是在 該租賃週期的最後一天（Last day of the leasing period） 到期給付（因為租客享有先使用後結算收益之法理推定）。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 雖實務常約定月初預付，但若合約未明載，法律不推定為期初預繳。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 月中支付無任何法定依據。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 租金給付義務依租賃週期屆滿產生，非取決於房東隨機之書面催告。
Question 25
An investor owns several commercial and residential rental assets. One property is leased under a Gross Lease, another under a Percentage Lease, and a third under a Triple Net (NNN) Lease. Regarding the operational expense allocations inherent in these lease structures, which of the following is NOT true?
投資人持有若干商業與住宅出租資產。一筆依總額租約（Gross Lease）、一筆依抽成租約（Percentage Lease）、一筆依三淨租約（NNN）。關於這些租約結構內含的營運費用分攤，下列何者不正確？
(A) Under a Gross Lease, the tenant pays a fixed flat rental sum, and the landlord remains obligated to pay all property taxes, hazard insurance, and structural repairs.
(B) Under a Triple Net (NNN) Lease, the tenant pays a base rent plus real estate taxes, property insurance, and ongoing common area maintenance costs.
(C) Under a Percentage Lease commonly used in retail centers, rent is calculated partly or entirely as a percentage of the gross sales receipts generated on the premises.
(D) Under a Triple Net (NNN) Lease, the commercial landlord retains the sole legal and financial responsibility to pay all building insurance and municipal property taxes without tenant reimbursement.
【正確答案】 (D)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (D) 解析： 在 三淨租約（Triple Net / NNN Lease） 架構下，承租人除支付固定基本租金外，契約明確約定由「承租人（Tenant）」直接負擔或向房東全額補貼所有的物業稅（Net Taxes）、建築保險費（Net Insurance）與維護營運費（Net Maintenance）。選項 (D) 聲稱房東承擔且不得要求租客補償是完全錯誤的，故為正確答案。
干擾選項 (A) 正確： 毛租約/總額租約（Gross Lease）由租客付固定租金，房東全額吸收營運維護與稅費。
干擾選項 (B) 正確： 三淨租約中租客承擔三大淨額開支（Taxes, Insurance, Maintenance）。
干擾選項 (C) 正確： 百分比/抽成租約（Percentage Lease）按租客在該商場的營業總額（Gross Sales）抽成計租。
Question 26
A tenant leased a single-family home under a written "Lease Option" agreement for a two-year term, paying a monthly rent of $3,500 along with an upfront, non-refundable cash option fee of $15,000 to the landlord. The agreement granted the tenant the exclusive right to purchase the property for $650,000 at any time prior to lease expiration. Regarding the legal nature of this agreement, which of the following is CORRECT?
租戶依書面「租賃附購買選擇權」（Lease Option）承租獨棟住宅兩年，每月租金 3,500 美元，並向房東預付不可退還的選擇權金 15,000 美元。協議賦予租戶在租期屆滿前隨時以 650,000 美元購買該屋的專屬權利。關於此協議的法律性質，下列何者正確？
(A) The contract is a bilateral agreement from inception, legally compelling the tenant to purchase the property at $650,000.
(B) The landlord is the Optionee and the tenant is the Optionor bound by a personal service employment contract.
(C) The contract begins as a unilateral contract binding only the optionor, and converts into an enforceable bilateral sales contract once the tenant exercises the option.
(D) The agreement is voidable at the option of the landlord because the $15,000 fee constitutes illegal unearned consideration under California law.
【正確答案】 (C)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (C) 解析： 附買回選擇權租約（Lease Option） 在簽約時為 單務契約（Unilateral Contract），房東（Optionor 授與人）受定金對價約束，承諾在期限內只要租客決定購買就必須出售；而租客（Optionee 受領人）保有買或不買的自由選擇權。一旦租客正式行使選擇權（Exercise the option），該合約立即轉變為具雙向拘束力的「雙務買賣契約（Bilateral Sales Contract）」。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 簽約初期對租客而言無強制購買義務，非雙務合約。
干擾選項 (B) 錯誤： 房東為 Optionor（授與人），租客為 Optionee（受領人）。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 選擇權必須有實質對價（Valuable consideration）支持，非退還之 Option fee 完全合法有效。
Question 27
An owner of an unencumbered parcel of land granted an electric utility corporation the recorded right to erect high-voltage transmission towers and string power cables across the northern boundary of the property. Years later, the owner subdivided and conveyed the parcel to various residential buyers. With respect to the utility company's legal interest, which of the following statements is CORRECT?
無負擔土地所有權人授與電力公用事業已登記之權利，得於該地北界架設高壓電塔並拉設電纜。數年後，所有權人將該地分割讓與多名住宅買方。關於電力公司的法律權益，下列敘述何者正確？
(A) The utility company holds an appurtenant dominant tenement that automatically merged upon residential subdivision.
(B) The utility company holds an easement in gross, which represents a personal or commercial interest burdening the land without a benefited dominant parcel.
(C) The utility rights were extinguished upon sale of the underlying property because licenses do not run with the land.
(D) The utility company holds a non-possessory license that may be terminated at will by any of the new residential grantees.
【正確答案】 (B)
【詳細深度解析】
正確選項 (B) 解析： 公用事業機構（如電力、自來水、瓦斯公司）跨越私人土地架設管線所取得的權益屬於 個人地役權 / 無需役地地役權（Easement in Gross）。此類地役權僅有受物權負擔之「供役地（Servient Tenement）」，並無相鄰獲益之「需役地（Dominant Tenement）」，權利專屬於公用事業實體，在土地分割過戶後仍持續附隨負擔該土地。
干擾選項 (A) 錯誤： 該權益並無獲益之需役地，非附屬地役權（Appurtenant easement）。
干擾選項 (C) 錯誤： 已登記之商業個人地役權為實質物權，非隨意撤銷之許可（License），不因土地轉售而消滅。
干擾選項 (D) 錯誤： 地役權非許可權，新地主無權任意將其終止。
Question 28
